- **Effect AI**: This skill should be used when the user asks about "Effect AI", "@effect/ai", "LLM integration", "AI tool use", "AI execution planning", "building AI agents", "AI providers", "structured AI output", "AI completions", "Effect OpenAI", "Effect Anthropic", or needs to understand how Effect integrates with AI/LLM services. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data/tree/HEAD/ai-llm/effect-ai) · ★30 · 2 copies)
- **Effect Estimate Explainer**: This skill explains what a research effect estimate means and what it does not: risk ratios versus odds ratios, the proportional hazards assumption behind a hazard ratio, number needed to treat, standardized mean differences, and the role of the confidence interval. It is a teaching and interpretation-support tool for researchers and students; it does not interpret real patient results and does not advise on treatment. ([salikkhann/openmedskills](https://github.com/salikkhann/openmedskills/tree/HEAD/skills/biostatistics/effect-estimate-explainer) · ★5)
- **Effect TypeScript Standard Library for Production Applications**: Effect is a comprehensive TypeScript standard library that provides structured concurrency, typed errors, dependency injection, schema validation, and observability — replacing dozens of individual packages with one cohesive, type-safe framework for building production-grade applications. ([agentskillexchange/skills](https://github.com/agentskillexchange/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/effect-typescript-standard-library-production) · ★65)
- **effect-abstraction-audit**: Use when analyzing the Reactive Agents codebase for architectural improvements, abstraction opportunities, composability gaps, or Effect-TS engineering quality — before proposing refactors, during design reviews, or when codebase complexity is growing.
